==== project/app/admin/page.tsx ====
'use client';

import { useEffect, useState, useCallback } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { Shield, Check, X, AlertCircle, Archive, Eye } from 'lucide-react';
import { useAuth } from '@/lib/auth-context';
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase/client';
import { DashboardShell } from '@/components/dashboard-shell';
import { Property, PROPERTY_TYPE_LABELS, PROPERTY_STATUS_LABELS, formatPrice, isAdminRole, ModerationAction } from '@/lib/types';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge';
import { Textarea } from '@/components/ui/textarea';
import { toast } from 'sonner';
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils';

export default function AdminPage() {
  const { user, profile, loading } = useAuth();
  const router = useRouter();
  const [pending, setPending] = useState<Property[]>([]);
  const [allProperties, setAllProperties] = useState<Property[]>([]);
  const [dataLoading, setDataLoading] = useState(true);
  const [selectedProperty, setSelectedProperty] = useState<Property | null>(null);
  const [moderationNote, setModerationNote] = useState('');

  const fetchData = useCallback(async () => {
    const [pendingRes, allRes] = await Promise.all([
      supabase.from('properties').select('*').eq('status', 'PENDING_REVIEW').order('created_at', { ascending: false }),
      supabase.from('properties').select('*').order('created_at', { ascending: false }).limit(50),
    ]);
    setPending((pendingRes.data as Property[]) || []);
    setAllProperties((allRes.data as Property[]) || []);
    setDataLoading(false);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!loading && !user) router.push('/auth/entrar');
  }, [loading, user, router]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!loading && user && !isAdminRole(profile?.role)) {
      router.push('/dashboard');
    }
  }, [loading, user, profile, router]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (user && isAdminRole(profile?.role)) fetchData();
  }, [user, profile, fetchData]);

  async function handleModeration(property: Property, action: ModerationAction) {
    if (!user) return;

    const newStatus = action === 'APPROVED' ? 'PUBLISHED' :
      action === 'REJECTED' ? 'ARCHIVED' :
      action === 'SUSPENDED' ? 'SUSPENDED' :
      action === 'ARCHIVED' ? 'ARCHIVED' :
      'CHANGES_REQUESTED';

    const { error } = await supabase
      .from('properties')
      .update({ status: newStatus, published_at: action === 'APPROVED' ? new Date().toISOString() : null })
      .eq('id', property.id);

    if (error) {
      toast.error('Erro ao moderar imóvel');
      return;
    }

    await supabase.from('moderation_logs').insert({
      property_id: property.id,
      moderator_id: user.id,
      action,
      notes: moderationNote || null,
    });

    await supabase.from('notifications').insert({
      user_id: property.owner_id,
      type: action === 'APPROVED' ? 'PROPERTY_APPROVED' : action === 'REJECTED' ? 'PROPERTY_REJECTED' : 'PROPERTY_CHANGES_REQUESTED',
      title: action === 'APPROVED' ? 'Imóvel aprovado' : action === 'REJECTED' ? 'Imóvel rejeitado' : 'Alterações solicitadas',
      message: `O seu imóvel "${property.title}" foi ${action === 'APPROVED' ? 'aprovado e publicado' : action === 'REJECTED' ? 'rejeitado' : 'marcado para alterações'}.`,
      data: { property_id: property.id, action },
    });

    toast.success(`Imóvel ${action === 'APPROVED' ? 'aprovado' : action === 'REJECTED' ? 'rejeitado' : 'atualizado'}`);
    setSelectedProperty(null);
    setModerationNote('');
    fetchData();
  }

  if (loading || dataLoading) {
    return <DashboardShell><div className="h-8 w-48 animate-pulse rounded bg-muted" /></DashboardShell>;
  }

  return (
    <DashboardShell>
      <div className="mb-8 flex items-center gap-3">
        <Shield className="h-6 w-6 text-gold" />
        <div>
          <h1 className="font-serif text-2xl font-bold sm:text-3xl">Administração</h1>
          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground">Moderação e gestão de imóveis</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Pending Review */}
      <div className="mb-12">
        <div className="mb-4 flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-gold/10">
            <AlertCircle className="h-4 w-4 text-gold" />
          </div>
          <h2 className="font-serif text-lg font-medium">Aguardando aprovação</h2>
          <Badge variant="secondary" className="text-xs">{pending.length}</Badge>
        </div>

        {pending.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card p-8 text-center">
            <Check className="mx-auto h-8 w-8 text-muted-foreground/40" />
            <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-muted-foreground">Sem imóveis pendentes. Tudo em dia.</p>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="space-y-3">
            {pending.map((p) => (
              <div key={p.id} className="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card p-4">
                <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-start sm:justify-between">
                  <div className="flex items-start gap-3 min-w-0 flex-1">
                    {p.main_image_url && (
                      <img src={p.main_image_url} alt={p.title} className="h-20 w-20 rounded-sm object-cover" />
                    )}
                    <div className="min-w-0">
                      <Link href={`/imoveis/${p.slug}`} className="text-sm font-medium hover:text-gold">
                        {p.title}
                      </Link>
                      <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                        {PROPERTY_TYPE_LABELS[p.type]} • {formatPrice(p.price, p.currency)}
                      </p>
                      <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                        {p.neighborhood}, {p.municipality}, {p.province}
                      </p>
                      <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                        Publicado em {new Date(p.created_at).toLocaleDateString('pt-AO')}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                    <Button size="sm" onClick={() => handleModeration(p, 'APPROVED')} className="bg-green-600 text-white hover:bg-green-700">
                      <Check className="mr-1 h-4 w-4" /> Aprovar
                    </Button>
                    <Button size="sm" variant="outline" onClick={() => { setSelectedProperty(p); setModerationNote(''); }}>
                      <AlertCircle className="mr-1 h-4 w-4" /> Alterações
                    </Button>
                    <Button size="sm" variant="outline" onClick={() => handleModeration(p, 'REJECTED')}>
                      <X className="mr-1 h-4 w-4" /> Rejeitar
                    </Button>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {selectedProperty?.id === p.id && (
                  <div className="mt-4 border-t border-border pt-4">
                    <Textarea
                      placeholder="Notas para o proprietário sobre as alterações necessárias..."
                      value={moderationNote}
                      onChange={(e) => setModerationNote(e.target.value)}
                      rows={3}
                    />
                    <div className="mt-2 flex gap-2">
                      <Button size="sm" onClick={() => handleModeration(p, 'CHANGES_REQUESTED')}>
                        Solicitar alterações
                      </Button>
                      <Button size="sm" variant="outline" onClick={() => setSelectedProperty(null)}>
                        Cancelar
                      </Button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* All Properties */}
      <div>
        <h2 className="mb-4 font-serif text-lg font-medium">Todos os imóveis</h2>
        <div className="overflow-x-auto rounded-sm border border-border">
          <table className="w-full text-sm">
            <thead className="bg-muted/50">
              <tr>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 text-left text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground">Imóvel</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 text-left text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground hidden sm:table-cell">Tipo</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 text-left text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground hidden lg:table-cell">Localização</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 text-left text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground">Estado</th>
                <th className="px-4 py-3 text-left text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground">Ações</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {allProperties.map((p) => (
                <tr key={p.id} className="border-t border-border">
                  <td className="px-4 py-3">
                    <Link href={`/imoveis/${p.slug}`} className="font-medium hover:text-gold">
                      {p.title}
                    </Link>
                    <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{formatPrice(p.price, p.currency)}</p>
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3 hidden sm:table-cell text-muted-foreground">
                    {PROPERTY_TYPE_LABELS[p.type]}
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3 hidden lg:table-cell text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                    {p.neighborhood}, {p.province}
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3">
                    <Badge variant="outline" className="text-xs">
                      {PROPERTY_STATUS_LABELS[p.status]}
                    </Badge>
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-4 py-3">
                    <div className="flex gap-1">
                      <Link href={`/imoveis/${p.slug}`}>
                        <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" className="h-8 w-8 p-0">
                          <Eye className="h-4 w-4" />
                        </Button>
                      </Link>
                      {p.status === 'PUBLISHED' && (
                        <Button
                          size="sm"
                          variant="ghost"
                          className="h-8 w-8 p-0"
                          onClick={() => handleModeration(p, 'SUSPENDED')}
                        >
                          <Archive className="h-4 w-4" />
                        </Button>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </div>
    </DashboardShell>
  );
}


==== project/app/auth/entrar/page.tsx ====
'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { Mail, Lock, ArrowRight, ArrowLeft } from 'lucide-react';
import { useAuth } from '@/lib/auth-context';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';
import { Label } from '@/components/ui/label';
import { toast } from 'sonner';

export default function SignInPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const { signIn } = useAuth();
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  async function handleSubmit(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!email || !password) {
      toast.error('Preencha email e senha');
      return;
    }
    setLoading(true);
    const { error } = await signIn(email, password);
    setLoading(false);
    if (error) {
      toast.error(error);
    } else {
      toast.success('Sessão iniciada com sucesso');
      router.push('/dashboard');
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen flex-col lg:flex-row">
      {/* Left panel */}
      <div className="relative hidden lg:flex lg:flex-1 lg:flex-col lg:justify-end lg:overflow-hidden">
        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-b from-ink-900 to-ink-800" />
        <div className="relative z-10 p-12">
          <Link href="/" className="font-serif text-2xl font-bold text-white">
            KUBATA KIÉ
          </Link>
          <p className="mt-6 max-w-md font-serif text-2xl leading-relaxed text-white/80">
            A plataforma PropTech de Angola. A jornada imobiliária completa num só lugar.
          </p>
          <div className="mt-8 flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="h-px w-12 bg-gold" />
            <span className="text-xs uppercase tracking-widest text-gold">Bem-vindo de volta</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Right panel — form */}
      <div className="flex flex-1 items-center justify-center bg-background px-4 py-12">
        <div className="w-full max-w-md">
          <Link href="/" className="mb-8 inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground">
            <ArrowLeft className="h-4 w-4" />
            Voltar ao início
          </Link>

          <div className="lg:hidden mb-8">
            <h1 className="font-serif text-2xl font-bold">KUBATA KIÉ</h1>
          </div>

          <h2 className="font-serif text-3xl font-bold">Entrar</h2>
          <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
            Aceda à sua conta para gerir imóveis, mensagens e favoritos.
          </p>

          <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="mt-8 space-y-4">
            <div>
              <Label htmlFor="email">Email</Label>
              <div className="relative mt-1.5">
                <Mail className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 h-4 w-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-muted-foreground" />
                <Input
                  id="email"
                  type="email"
                  placeholder="seu@email.com"
                  value={email}
                  onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                  className="pl-10"
                  required
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <Label htmlFor="password">Senha</Label>
              <div className="relative mt-1.5">
                <Lock className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 h-4 w-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-muted-foreground" />
                <Input
                  id="password"
                  type="password"
                  placeholder="••••••••"
                  value={password}
                  onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                  className="pl-10"
                  required
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <Button
              type="submit"
              className="w-full bg-foreground text-background hover:bg-foreground/90"
              disabled={loading}
            >
              {loading ? 'A entrar...' : 'Entrar'}
              {!loading && <ArrowRight className="ml-2 h-4 w-4" />}
            </Button>
          </form>

          <p className="mt-6 text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground">
            Não tem conta?{' '}
            <Link href="/auth/registar" className="font-medium text-gold hover:underline">
              Criar conta
            </Link>
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


==== project/app/auth/registar/page.tsx ====
'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { Mail, Lock, User, Phone, ArrowRight, ArrowLeft, Check } from 'lucide-react';
import { useAuth } from '@/lib/auth-context';
import { UserRole } from '@/lib/types';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';
import { Label } from '@/components/ui/label';
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils';
import { toast } from 'sonner';

const ROLES: { value: UserRole; label: string; desc: string }[] = [
  { value: 'BUYER', label: 'Comprador', desc: 'Procuro comprar imóvel' },
  { value: 'TENANT', label: 'Inquilino', desc: 'Procuro arrendar imóvel' },
  { value: 'SELLER', label: 'Proprietário', desc: 'Quero vender ou arrendar' },
  { value: 'AGENT', label: 'Agente', desc: 'Sou agente imobiliário' },
];

export default function SignUpPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const { signUp } = useAuth();
  const [fullName, setFullName] = useState('');
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [role, setRole] = useState<UserRole>('BUYER');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  async function handleSubmit(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!fullName || !email || !password) {
      toast.error('Preencha todos os campos obrigatórios');
      return;
    }
    if (password.length < 6) {
      toast.error('A senha deve ter pelo menos 6 caracteres');
      return;
    }
    setLoading(true);
    const { error } = await signUp(email, password, fullName, role, phone || undefined);
    setLoading(false);
    if (error) {
      toast.error(error);
    } else {
      toast.success('Conta criada com sucesso');
      router.push('/dashboard');
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen flex-col lg:flex-row">
      {/* Left panel */}
      <div className="relative hidden lg:flex lg:flex-1 lg:flex-col lg:justify-end lg:overflow-hidden">
        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-b from-ink-900 to-ink-800" />
        <div className="relative z-10 p-12">
          <Link href="/" className="font-serif text-2xl font-bold text-white">
            KUBATA KIÉ
          </Link>
          <p className="mt-6 max-w-md font-serif text-2xl leading-relaxed text-white/80">
            Junte-se à plataforma PropTech que está a transformar o mercado imobiliário em Angola.
          </p>
          <div className="mt-8 flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="h-px w-12 bg-gold" />
            <span className="text-xs uppercase tracking-widest text-gold">Comece a sua jornada</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Right panel — form */}
      <div className="flex flex-1 items-center justify-center bg-background px-4 py-12">
        <div className="w-full max-w-md">
          <Link href="/" className="mb-8 inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground">
            <ArrowLeft className="h-4 w-4" />
            Voltar ao início
          </Link>

          <div className="lg:hidden mb-8">
            <h1 className="font-serif text-2xl font-bold">KUBATA KIÉ</h1>
          </div>

          <h2 className="font-serif text-3xl font-bold">Criar conta</h2>
          <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
            Escolha o seu perfil e comece a explorar imóveis em Angola.
          </p>

          <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="mt-8 space-y-5">
            {/* Role selection */}
            <div>
              <Label className="mb-2 block">Tipo de conta</Label>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
                {ROLES.map((r) => (
                  <button
                    key={r.value}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setRole(r.value)}
                    className={cn(
                      'relative rounded-sm border p-3 text-left transition-all',
                      role === r.value
                        ? 'border-gold bg-gold/5'
                        : 'border-border hover:border-foreground/20'
                    )}
                  >
                    {role === r.value && (
                      <Check className="absolute right-2 top-2 h-3.5 w-3.5 text-gold" />
                    )}
                    <p className="text-sm font-medium">{r.label}</p>
                    <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-muted-foreground">{r.desc}</p>
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <Label htmlFor="fullName">Nome completo</Label>
              <div className="relative mt-1.5">
                <User className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 h-4 w-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-muted-foreground" />
                <Input
                  id="fullName"
                  placeholder="O seu nome"
                  value={fullName}
                  onChange={(e) => setFullName(e.target.value)}
                  className="pl-10"
                  required
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <Label htmlFor="email">Email</Label>
              <div className="relative mt-1.5">
                <Mail className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 h-4 w-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-muted-foreground" />
                <Input
                  id="email"
                  type="email"
                  placeholder="seu@email.com"
                  value={email}
                  onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                  className="pl-10"
                  required
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <Label htmlFor="phone">Telefone <span className="text-muted-foreground">(opcional)</span></Label>
              <div className="relative mt-1.5">
                <Phone className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 h-4 w-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-muted-foreground" />
                <Input
                  id="phone"
                  placeholder="+244 ..."
                  value={phone}
                  onChange={(e) => setPhone(e.target.value)}
                  className="pl-10"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <Label htmlFor="password">Senha</Label>
              <div className="relative mt-1.5">
                <Lock className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 h-4 w-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-muted-foreground" />
                <Input
                  id="password"
                  type="password"
                  placeholder="Mínimo 6 caracteres"
                  value={password}
                  onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                  className="pl-10"
                  required
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <Button
              type="submit"
              className="w-full bg-foreground text-background hover:bg-foreground/90"
              disabled={loading}
            >
              {loading ? 'A criar conta...' : 'Criar conta'}
              {!loading && <ArrowRight className="ml-2 h-4 w-4" />}
            </Button>
          </form>

          <p className="mt-6 text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground">
            Já tem conta?{' '}
            <Link href="/auth/entrar" className="font-medium text-gold hover:underline">
              Entrar
            </Link>
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


==== project/app/dashboard/configuracoes/page.tsx ====
'use client';

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { useAuth } from '@/lib/auth-context';
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase/client';
import { DashboardShell } from '@/components/dashboard-shell';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';
import { Label } from '@/components/ui/label';
import { Textarea } from '@/components/ui/textarea';
import { toast } from 'sonner';

export default function SettingsPage() {
  const { user, profile, loading, refreshProfile } = useAuth();
  const router = useRouter();
  const [fullName, setFullName] = useState(profile?.full_name ?? '');
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState(profile?.phone ?? '');
  const [bio, setBio] = useState(profile?.bio ?? '');
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!loading && !user) router.push('/auth/entrar');
  }, [loading, user, router]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (profile) {
      setFullName(profile.full_name ?? '');
      setPhone(profile.phone ?? '');
      setBio(profile.bio ?? '');
    }
  }, [profile]);

  async function handleSave() {
    if (!user) return;
    setSaving(true);
    const { error } = await supabase
      .from('profiles')
      .update({ full_name: fullName, phone, bio, updated_at: new Date().toISOString() })
      .eq('id', user.id);

    setSaving(false);
    if (error) {
      toast.error('Erro ao guardar');
    } else {
      toast.success('Perfil atualizado');
      refreshProfile();
    }
  }

  if (loading) {
    return <DashboardShell><div className="h-8 w-48 animate-pulse rounded bg-muted" /></DashboardShell>;
  }

  return (
    <DashboardShell>
      <div className="mb-8">
        <h1 className="font-serif text-2xl font-bold sm:text-3xl">Configurações</h1>
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground">Gerir o seu perfil e preferências.</p>
      </div>

      <div className="max-w-2xl space-y-6">
        {/* Profile */}
        <div className="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card p-6">
          <h2 className="mb-4 font-serif text-lg font-medium">Perfil</h2>
          <div className="space-y-4">
            <div>
              <Label htmlFor="name" className="text-xs">Nome completo</Label>
              <Input id="name" value={fullName} onChange={(e) => setFullName(e.target.value)} className="mt-1.5" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <Label htmlFor="email" className="text-xs">Email</Label>
              <Input id="email" value={profile?.email ?? ''} disabled className="mt-1.5 bg-muted/50" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <Label htmlFor="phone" className="text-xs">Telefone</Label>
              <Input id="phone" value={phone} onChange={(e) => setPhone(e.target.value)} placeholder="+244 ..." className="mt-1.5" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <Label htmlFor="bio" className="text-xs">Bio</Label>
              <Textarea id="bio" value={bio} onChange={(e) => setBio(e.target.value)} rows={3} placeholder="Fale sobre si..." className="mt-1.5" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <Label className="text-xs">Tipo de conta</Label>
              <div className="mt-1.5 rounded-sm bg-muted/50 px-3 py-2 text-sm">{profile?.role}</div>
            </div>
            <Button onClick={handleSave} disabled={saving} className="bg-foreground text-background hover:bg-foreground/90">
              {saving ? 'A guardar...' : 'Guardar alterações'}
            </Button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Preferences */}
        <div className="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card p-6">
          <h2 className="mb-4 font-serif text-lg font-medium">Preferências</h2>
          <div className="space-y-3 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
            <p>Idioma: Português</p>
            <p>Moeda: Kwanza (Kz)</p>
            <p>Notificações: Ativas</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </DashboardShell>
  );
}

==== project/app/dashboard/favoritos/page.tsx ====
'use client';

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { Heart, Search } from 'lucide-react';
import { useAuth } from '@/lib/auth-context';
import { useFavorites } from '@/lib/use-favorites';
import { DashboardShell } from '@/components/dashboard-shell';
import { PropertyCard, PropertyCardSkeleton } from '@/components/property-card';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';

export default function DashboardFavoritesPage() {
  const { user, loading } = useAuth();
  const router = useRouter();
  const { favorites, loading: favLoading } = useFavorites();

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!loading && !user) router.push('/auth/entrar');
  }, [loading, user, router]);

  if (loading || favLoading) {
    return (
      <DashboardShell>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
          {Array.from({ length: 3 }).map((_, i) => <PropertyCardSkeleton key={i} />)}
        </div>
      </DashboardShell>
    );
  }

  return (
    <DashboardShell>
      <div className="mb-8">
        <h1 className="font-serif text-2xl font-bold sm:text-3xl">Favoritos</h1>
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground">{favorites.length} imóveis guardados</p>
      </div>

      {favorites.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-sm border border-border bg-card py-20 text-center">
          <Heart className="h-12 w-12 text-muted-foreground/40" />
          <h3 className="mt-4 font-serif text-lg font-medium">Sem favoritos</h3>
          <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">Explore o marketplace e guarde os imóveis que lhe interessam.</p>
          <Link href="/imoveis" className="mt-4">
            <Button className="bg-gold text-white hover:bg-gold/90">
              <Search className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" />
              Explorar
            </Button>
          </Link>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
          {favorites.map((fav) => fav.property && <PropertyCard key={fav.id} property={fav.property} />)}
        </div>
      )}
    </DashboardShell>
  );
}


==== project/app/dashboard/imoveis/page.tsx ====
'use client';

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { Home, PlusCircle, Eye, MessageSquare } from 'lucide-react';
import { useAuth } from '@/lib/auth-context';
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase/client';
import { DashboardShell } from '@/components/dashboard-shell';
import { Property, PROPERTY_STATUS_LABELS, PROPERTY_STATUS_COLORS, formatPrice } from '@/lib/types';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge';
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils';

export default function MyPropertiesPage() {
  const { user, loading } = useAuth();
  const router = useRouter();
  const [properties, setProperties] = useState<Property[]>([]);
  const [dataLoading, setDataLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!loading && !user) router.push('/auth/entrar');
  }, [loading, user, router]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!user) return;
    supabase
      .from('properties')
      .select('*')
      .eq('owner_id', user.id)
      .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
      .then(({ data }) => {
        setProperties((data as Property[]) || []);
        setDataLoading(false);
      });
  }, [user]);

  if (loading || dataLoading) {
    return <DashboardShell><div className="h-8 w-48 animate-pulse rounded bg-muted" /></DashboardShell>;
  }

  return (
    <DashboardShell>
      <div className="mb-8 flex items-center justify-between">
        <div>
          <h1 className="font-serif text-2xl font-bold sm:text-3xl">Meus imóveis</h1>
          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground">{properties.length} imóveis publicados</p>
        </div>
        <Link href="/publicar">
          <Button className="bg-gold text-white hover:bg-gold/90">
            <PlusCircle className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" />
            Publicar
          </Button>
        </Link>
      </div>

      {properties.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-sm border border-border bg-card py-20 text-center">
          <Home className="h-12 w-12 text-muted-foreground/40" />
          <h3 className="mt-4 font-serif text-lg font-medium">Nenhum imóvel publicado</h3>
          <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">Comece por publicar o seu primeiro imóvel.</p>
          <Link href="/publicar" className="mt-4">
            <Button className="bg-gold text-white hover:bg-gold/90">Publicar imóvel</Button>
          </Link>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-3">
          {properties.map((p) => (
            <div
              key={p.id}
              className="flex flex-col gap-3 rounded-sm border border-border bg-card p-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between"
            >
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3 min-w-0 flex-1">
                {p.main_image_url && (
                  <img src={p.main_image_url} alt={p.title} className="h-16 w-16 rounded-sm object-cover" />
                )}
                <div className="min-w-0">
                  <Link href={`/imoveis/${p.slug}`} className="text-sm font-medium hover:text-gold">
                    {p.title}
                  </Link>
                  <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                    {formatPrice(p.price, p.currency)}
                  </p>
                  <div className="mt-1 flex items-center gap-3 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                    <span className="flex items-center gap-1"><Eye className="h-3 w-3" /> {p.view_count}</span>
                    <span className="flex items-center gap-1"><MessageSquare className="h-3 w-3" /> {p.inquiry_count}</span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <Badge className={cn('text-xs', PROPERTY_STATUS_COLORS[p.status])}>
                  {PROPERTY_STATUS_LABELS[p.status]}
                </Badge>
                <Link href={`/imoveis/${p.slug}`}>
                  <Button size="sm" variant="outline">Ver</Button>
                </Link>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </DashboardShell>
  );
}


==== project/app/dashboard/mensagens/page.tsx ====
'use client';

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { MessageSquare } from 'lucide-react';
import { useAuth } from '@/lib/auth-context';
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase/client';
import { DashboardShell } from '@/components/dashboard-shell';
import { Inquiry, PROPERTY_TYPE_LABELS } from '@/lib/types';

export default function MessagesPage() {
  const { user, loading } = useAuth();
  const router = useRouter();
  const [inquiries, setInquiries] = useState<Inquiry[]>([]);
  const [dataLoading, setDataLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!loading && !user) router.push('/auth/entrar');
  }, [loading, user, router]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!user) return;
    supabase
      .from('inquiries')
      .select('*, property:properties(title, slug, type)')
      .or(`user_id.eq.${user.id},recipient_id.eq.${user.id}`)
      .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
      .then(({ data }) => {
        setInquiries((data as unknown as Inquiry[]) || []);
        setDataLoading(false);
      });
  }, [user]);

  if (loading || dataLoading) {
    return <DashboardShell><div className="h-8 w-48 animate-pulse rounded bg-muted" /></DashboardShell>;
  }

  return (
    <DashboardShell>
      <div className="mb-8">
        <h1 className="font-serif text-2xl font-bold sm:text-3xl">Mensagens</h1>
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground">{inquiries.length} mensagens</p>
      </div>

      {inquiries.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-sm border border-border bg-card py-20 text-center">
          <MessageSquare className="h-12 w-12 text-muted-foreground/40" />
          <h3 className="mt-4 font-serif text-lg font-medium">Sem mensagens</h3>
          <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">As mensagens sobre os seus imóveis aparecem aqui.</p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-3">
          {inquiries.map((inq) => (
            <div key={inq.id} className="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card p-4">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                  {inq.property?.title || 'Imóvel'}
                </span>
                <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                  {new Date(inq.created_at).toLocaleDateString('pt-AO')}
                </span>
              </div>
              <p className="mt-2 text-sm">{inq.message}</p>
              {inq.name && (
                <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                  De: {inq.name} {inq.phone && `• ${inq.phone}`}
                </p>
              )}
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </DashboardShell>
  );
}


==== project/app/dashboard/page.tsx ====
'use client';

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { Home, MessageSquare, Calendar, Heart, TrendingUp, PlusCircle, Eye } from 'lucide-react';
import { useAuth } from '@/lib/auth-context';
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase/client';
import { DashboardShell } from '@/components/dashboard-shell';
import { Property, Inquiry, ViewingRequest } from '@/lib/types';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { formatPrice, PROPERTY_STATUS_LABELS, PROPERTY_STATUS_COLORS } from '@/lib/types';
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge';
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils';

export default function DashboardPage() {
  const { user, profile, loading } = useAuth();
  const router = useRouter();
  const [stats, setStats] = useState({
    totalListings: 0,
    activeListings: 0,
    totalInquiries: 0,
    totalViewings: 0,
    totalFavorites: 0,
    totalViews: 0,
  });
  const [recentProperties, setRecentProperties] = useState<Property[]>([]);
  const [recentInquiries, setRecentInquiries] = useState<Inquiry[]>([]);
  const [recentViewings, setRecentViewings] = useState<ViewingRequest[]>([]);
  const [dataLoading, setDataLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!loading && !user) {
      router.push('/auth/entrar');
    }
  }, [loading, user, router]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!user) return;
    let cancelled = false;

    async function fetchData() {
      const [propsRes, inquiriesRes, viewingsRes, favsRes] = await Promise.all([
        supabase.from('properties').select('*').eq('owner_id', user!.id).order('created_at', { ascending: false }),
        supabase.from('inquiries').select('*, property:properties(title, slug)').eq('recipient_id', user!.id).order('created_at', { ascending: false }).limit(5),
        supabase.from('viewing_requests').select('*, property:properties(title, slug)').eq('agent_id', user!.id).order('created_at', { ascending: false }).limit(5),
        supabase.from('favorites').select('id', { count: 'exact', head: true }).eq('user_id', user!.id),
      ]);

      if (cancelled) return;

      const properties = (propsRes.data as Property[]) || [];
      setRecentProperties(properties.slice(0, 5));
      setRecentInquiries((inquiriesRes.data as unknown as Inquiry[]) || []);
      setRecentViewings((viewingsRes.data as unknown as ViewingRequest[]) || []);

      setStats({
        totalListings: properties.length,
        activeListings: properties.filter((p) => p.status === 'PUBLISHED').length,
        totalInquiries: inquiriesRes.data?.length || 0,
        totalViewings: viewingsRes.data?.length || 0,
        totalFavorites: favsRes.count || 0,
        totalViews: properties.reduce((sum, p) => sum + (p.view_count || 0), 0),
      });
      setDataLoading(false);
    }

    fetchData();
    return () => { cancelled = true; };
  }, [user]);

  if (loading || dataLoading) {
    return (
      <DashboardShell>
        <div className="space-y-4">
          <div className="h-8 w-48 animate-pulse rounded bg-muted" />
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 lg:grid-cols-4">
            {Array.from({ length: 4 }).map((_, i) => (
              <div key={i} className="h-28 animate-pulse rounded-sm bg-muted" />
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      </DashboardShell>
    );
  }

  const statCards = [
    { label: 'Imóveis ativos', value: stats.activeListings, icon: Home, href: '/dashboard/imoveis' },
    { label: 'Total imóveis', value: stats.totalListings, icon: PlusCircle, href: '/dashboard/imoveis' },
    { label: 'Mensagens', value: stats.totalInquiries, icon: MessageSquare, href: '/dashboard/mensagens' },
    { label: 'Visitas', value: stats.totalViewings, icon: Calendar, href: '/dashboard/visitas' },
    { label: 'Favoritos', value: stats.totalFavorites, icon: Heart, href: '/dashboard/favoritos' },
    { label: 'Total visualizações', value: stats.totalViews, icon: Eye, href: '/dashboard/estatisticas' },
  ];

  return (
    <DashboardShell>
      <div className="mb-8">
        <h1 className="font-serif text-2xl font-bold sm:text-3xl">
          Olá, {profile?.full_name?.split(' ')[0] || 'utilizador'}
        </h1>
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
          Bem-vindo ao seu painel. Aqui pode gerir os seus imóveis, mensagens e visitas.
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* Stats */}
      <div className="mb-8 grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 lg:grid-cols-3 xl:grid-cols-6">
        {statCards.map((stat) => {
          const Icon = stat.icon;
          return (
            <Link
              key={stat.label}
              href={stat.href}
              className="group rounded-sm border border-border bg-card p-4 transition-all hover:border-gold/40 hover:shadow-sm"
            >
              <Icon className="h-5 w-5 text-gold" />
              <p className="mt-3 text-2xl font-bold font-serif">{stat.value}</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{stat.label}</p>
            </Link>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      {/* Quick action */}
      <div className="mb-8 flex items-center gap-3 rounded-sm border border-gold/20 bg-gold/5 p-4">
        <PlusCircle className="h-5 w-5 text-gold" />
        <p className="text-sm">Tem um novo imóvel para publicar?</p>
        <Link href="/publicar" className="ml-auto">
          <Button size="sm" className="bg-gold text-white hover:bg-gold/90">
            Publicar imóvel
          </Button>
        </Link>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-8 lg:grid-cols-2">
        {/* Recent properties */}
        <div>
          <div className="mb-4 flex items-center justify-between">
            <h2 className="font-serif text-lg font-medium">Os meus imóveis</h2>
            <Link href="/dashboard/imoveis" className="text-xs text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground">
              Ver todos
            </Link>
          </div>
          {recentProperties.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card p-8 text-center">
              <Home className="mx-auto h-8 w-8 text-muted-foreground/40" />
              <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-muted-foreground">Ainda não publicou imóveis</p>
              <Link href="/publicar" className="mt-3 inline-block">
                <Button size="sm" variant="outline">Publicar agora</Button>
              </Link>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="space-y-3">
              {recentProperties.map((p) => (
                <Link
                  key={p.id}
                  href={`/imoveis/${p.slug}`}
                  className="flex items-center justify-between rounded-sm border border-border bg-card p-3 transition-all hover:border-gold/30"
                >
                  <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                    <p className="truncate text-sm font-medium">{p.title}</p>
                    <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{formatPrice(p.price, p.currency)}</p>
                  </div>
                  <Badge className={cn('text-xs', PROPERTY_STATUS_COLORS[p.status])}>
                    {PROPERTY_STATUS_LABELS[p.status]}
                  </Badge>
                </Link>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Recent inquiries */}
        <div>
          <div className="mb-4 flex items-center justify-between">
            <h2 className="font-serif text-lg font-medium">Mensagens recentes</h2>
            <Link href="/dashboard/mensagens" className="text-xs text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground">
              Ver todas
            </Link>
          </div>
          {recentInquiries.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card p-8 text-center">
              <MessageSquare className="mx-auto h-8 w-8 text-muted-foreground/40" />
              <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-muted-foreground">Sem mensagens</p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="space-y-3">
              {recentInquiries.map((inq) => (
                <div key={inq.id} className="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card p-3">
                  <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground truncate">
                    {inq.property?.title || 'Imóvel'}
                  </p>
                  <p className="mt-1 text-sm line-clamp-1">{inq.message}</p>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </DashboardShell>
  );
}


==== project/app/dashboard/visitas/page.tsx ====
'use client';

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { Calendar } from 'lucide-react';
import { useAuth } from '@/lib/auth-context';
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase/client';
import { DashboardShell } from '@/components/dashboard-shell';
import { ViewingRequest } from '@/lib/types';
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge';

const VIEWING_STATUS_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
  REQUESTED: 'Pedido',
  CONFIRMED: 'Confirmada',
  RESCHEDULED: 'Reagendada',
  COMPLETED: 'Concluída',
  CANCELLED: 'Cancelada',
  NO_SHOW: 'Não compareceu',
};

export default function VisitsPage() {
  const { user, loading } = useAuth();
  const router = useRouter();
  const [viewings, setViewings] = useState<ViewingRequest[]>([]);
  const [dataLoading, setDataLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!loading && !user) router.push('/auth/entrar');
  }, [loading, user, router]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!user) return;
    supabase
      .from('viewing_requests')
      .select('*, property:properties(title, slug)')
      .or(`user_id.eq.${user.id},agent_id.eq.${user.id}`)
      .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
      .then(({ data }) => {
        setViewings((data as unknown as ViewingRequest[]) || []);
        setDataLoading(false);
      });
  }, [user]);

  if (loading || dataLoading) {
    return <DashboardShell><div className="h-8 w-48 animate-pulse rounded bg-muted" /></DashboardShell>;
  }

  return (
    <DashboardShell>
      <div className="mb-8">
        <h1 className="font-serif text-2xl font-bold sm:text-3xl">Visitas</h1>
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground">{viewings.length} pedidos de visita</p>
      </div>

      {viewings.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-sm border border-border bg-card py-20 text-center">
          <Calendar className="h-12 w-12 text-muted-foreground/40" />
          <h3 className="mt-4 font-serif text-lg font-medium">Sem visitas agendadas</h3>
          <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">Os pedidos de visita aos seus imóveis aparecem aqui.</p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-3">
          {viewings.map((v) => (
            <div key={v.id} className="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card p-4">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <span className="text-sm font-medium">{v.property?.title || 'Imóvel'}</span>
                <Badge variant="outline" className="text-xs">
                  {VIEWING_STATUS_LABELS[v.status] || v.status}
                </Badge>
              </div>
              <div className="mt-2 flex items-center gap-3 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                <span className="flex items-center gap-1">
                  <Calendar className="h-3 w-3" />
                  {new Date(v.preferred_date).toLocaleDateString('pt-AO')} às {v.preferred_time}
                </span>
              </div>
              {v.notes && <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">{v.notes}</p>}
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </DashboardShell>
  );
}


==== project/app/favoritos/page.tsx ====
'use client';

import Link from 'next/link';
import { Heart, Search } from 'lucide-react';
import { useFavorites } from '@/lib/use-favorites';
import { useAuth } from '@/lib/auth-context';
import { SiteHeader } from '@/components/site-header';
import { PropertyCard, PropertyCardSkeleton } from '@/components/property-card';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';

export default function FavoritesPage() {
  const { user, loading: authLoading } = useAuth();
  const { favorites, loading } = useFavorites();

  if (authLoading) {
    return (
      <>
        <SiteHeader />
        <div className="container-mw container-px py-20">
          <div className="h-8 w-48 animate-pulse rounded bg-muted" />
        </div>
      </>
    );
  }

  if (!user) {
    return (
      <>
        <SiteHeader />
        <div className="container-mw container-px py-20">
          <div className="mx-auto max-w-md text-center">
            <div className="mx-auto flex h-16 w-16 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-muted">
              <Heart className="h-8 w-8 text-muted-foreground" />
            </div>
            <h1 className="mt-6 font-serif text-2xl font-bold">Inicie sessão para ver favoritos</h1>
            <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
              Os seus imóveis favoritos aparecem aqui. Crie uma conta ou entre para os guardar.
            </p>
            <div className="mt-6 flex justify-center gap-3">
              <Link href="/auth/entrar">
                <Button variant="outline">Entrar</Button>
              </Link>
              <Link href="/auth/registar">
                <Button className="bg-gold text-white hover:bg-gold/90">Criar conta</Button>
              </Link>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </>
    );
  }

  return (
    <>
      <SiteHeader />
      <div className="container-mw container-px py-8 lg:py-12">
        <div className="mb-8">
          <div className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="h-px w-8 bg-gold" />
            <span className="text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-gold">
              A sua seleção
            </span>
          </div>
          <h1 className="font-serif text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight sm:text-4xl">
            Imóveis favoritos
          </h1>
          <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
            {loading ? 'A carregar...' : `${favorites.length} imóveis guardados`}
          </p>
        </div>

        {loading ? (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 xl:grid-cols-4">
            {Array.from({ length: 4 }).map((_, i) => (
              <PropertyCardSkeleton key={i} />
            ))}
          </div>
        ) : favorites.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-sm border border-border bg-card py-20 text-center">
            <div className="flex h-16 w-16 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-muted">
              <Heart className="h-8 w-8 text-muted-foreground" />
            </div>
            <h3 className="mt-6 font-serif text-xl font-medium">Sem favoritos</h3>
            <p className="mt-2 max-w-sm text-sm text-muted-foreground">
              Explore o marketplace e toque no coração para guardar os imóveis que lhe interessam.
            </p>
            <Link href="/imoveis" className="mt-6">
              <Button className="bg-gold text-white hover:bg-gold/90">
                <Search className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" />
                Explorar imóveis
              </Button>
            </Link>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 xl:grid-cols-4">
            {favorites.map((fav, i) => (
              <div key={fav.id} className="animate-fade-in-up" style={{ animationDelay: `${i * 40}ms` }}>
                {fav.property && <PropertyCard property={fav.property} />}
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

==== project/app/imoveis/[slug]/page.tsx ====
'use client';

import { useState, useEffect, use } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import Image from 'next/image';
import { notFound } from 'next/navigation';
import {
  Heart, Share2, MapPin, Bed, Bath, Maximize, Calendar, Phone,
  Check, ChevronLeft, ChevronRight, Eye, Home, Building, Car,
  Trees, Waves, Camera, Shield, Zap, Droplets, Wifi, Wind,
  Sofa, Fence, Sun, Mountain, Refrigerator, DoorOpen,
  Mail, MessageSquare, ArrowLeft, User,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase/client';
import { Property, Profile, formatPrice, formatArea, PROPERTY_TYPE_LABELS, PURPOSE_LABELS, AMENITY_LABELS, isPropertyAvailable, isPropertyReservedOrSold } from '@/lib/types';
import { useFavorites } from '@/lib/use-favorites';
import { useAuth } from '@/lib/auth-context';
import { SiteHeader } from '@/components/site-header';
import { ContactSellerDialog } from '@/components/contact-seller-dialog';
import { PropertyCard } from '@/components/property-card';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge';
import { toast } from 'sonner';
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils';

const AMENITY_ICONS: Record<string, React.ComponentType<{ className?: string }>> = {
  garage: Car,
  pool: Waves,
  garden: Trees,
  balcony: Mountain,
  ac: Wind,
  generator: Zap,
  security: Shield,
  furnished: Sofa,
  elevator: Building,
  parking: Car,
  water: Droplets,
  electricity: Zap,
  internet: Wifi,
  CCTV: Camera,
  fence: Fence,
  borehole: Droplets,
  solar: Sun,
  sea_view: Mountain,
  city_view: Mountain,
  kitchen: Refrigerator,
  wardrobe: DoorOpen,
};

interface PageProps {
  params: Promise<{ slug: string }>;
}

export default function PropertyDetailPage({ params }: PageProps) {
  const { slug } = use(params);
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const { isFavorite, toggleFavorite } = useFavorites();
  const [property, setProperty] = useState<Property | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [activeImage, setActiveImage] = useState(0);
  const [showContact, setShowContact] = useState(false);
  const [showGallery, setShowGallery] = useState(false);
  const [similar, setSimilar] = useState<Property[]>([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;

    async function fetchProperty() {
      setLoading(true);
      const { data, error } = await supabase
        .from('properties')
        .select(
          `*,
          owner:profiles!properties_owner_id_fkey(id, full_name, email, phone, avatar_url, role, bio, created_at),
          agent:profiles!properties_agent_id_fkey(id, full_name, email, phone, avatar_url, role, bio)
        `
        )
        .eq('slug', slug)
        .maybeSingle();

      if (cancelled) return;

      if (error) {
        setError('Erro ao carregar imóvel');
        setLoading(false);
        return;
      }

      if (!data) {
        setError('Imóvel não encontrado');
        setLoading(false);
        return;
      }

      setProperty(data as unknown as Property);
      setLoading(false);

      // Track view
      if (data && typeof window !== 'undefined') {
        supabase.from('property_view_events').insert({
          property_id: data.id,
          user_id: user?.id || null,
          session_id: sessionStorage.getItem('sessionId') || undefined,
        }).then();

        // Increment view count
        supabase.rpc('increment_view_count', { property_id: data.id }).then();
      }

      // Fetch similar properties
      if (data) {
        const { data: similarData } = await supabase
          .from('properties')
          .select('*, owner:profiles!properties_owner_id_fkey(id, full_name, avatar_url)')
          .eq('status', 'PUBLISHED')
          .eq('type', data.type)
          .neq('id', data.id)
          .limit(3)
          .order('created_at', { ascending: false });

        if (!cancelled && similarData) {
          setSimilar(similarData as unknown as Property[]);
        }
      }
    }

    fetchProperty();
    return () => { cancelled = true; };
  }, [slug, user?.id]);

  const handleFavorite = async () => {
    if (!property) return;
    const { error } = await toggleFavorite(property.id);
    if (error) toast.error(error);
    else toast.success(isFavorite(property.id) ? 'Removido dos favoritos' : 'Adicionado aos favoritos');
  };

  const handleShare = async () => {
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && property) {
      try {
        if (navigator.share) {
          await navigator.share({
            title: property.title,
            text: `KUBATA KIÉ — ${property.title}`,
            url: window.location.href,
          });
        } else {
          await navigator.clipboard.writeText(window.location.href);
          toast.success('Link copiado');
        }
      } catch {
        // user cancelled share
      }
    }
  };

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <>
        <SiteHeader />
        <div className="container-mw container-px py-20">
          <div className="aspect-[16/9] animate-pulse rounded-sm bg-muted" />
          <div className="mt-8 space-y-4">
            <div className="h-8 w-2/3 animate-pulse rounded bg-muted" />
            <div className="h-4 w-1/3 animate-pulse rounded bg-muted" />
            <div className="h-32 animate-pulse rounded bg-muted" />
          </div>
        </div>
      </>
    );
  }

  if (error || !property) {
    return (
      <>
        <SiteHeader />
        <div className="container-mw container-px py-20 text-center">
          <p className="font-serif text-2xl">{error || 'Imóvel não encontrado'}</p>
          <Link href="/imoveis" className="mt-4 inline-block">
            <Button variant="outline">Voltar aos imóveis</Button>
          </Link>
        </div>
      </>
    );
  }

  const allImages = [property.main_image_url, ...(property.images || [])].filter(Boolean) as string[];
  const soldOrRented = isPropertyReservedOrSold(property.status);
  const ownerProfile = property.owner as Profile | undefined;

  return (
    <>
      <SiteHeader />

      <div className="container-mw container-px py-6 lg:py-10">
        {/* Breadcrumb */}
        <nav className="mb-6 flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
          <Link href="/" className="hover:text-foreground">Início</Link>
          <span>/</span>
          <Link href="/imoveis" className="hover:text-foreground">Imóveis</Link>
          <span>/</span>
          <Link href={`/imoveis/${property.purpose === 'SALE' ? 'comprar' : 'arrendar'}`} className="hover:text-foreground">
            {PURPOSE_LABELS[property.purpose]}
          </Link>
          <span>/</span>
          <span className="truncate text-foreground">{property.title}</span>
        </nav>

        {/* Title & Status */}
        <div className="mb-6 flex flex-col gap-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-start sm:justify-between">
          <div>
            <div className="mb-2 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
              <Badge variant="secondary" className="text-xs">
                {PROPERTY_TYPE_LABELS[property.type]}
              </Badge>
              <Badge variant="outline" className="text-xs">
                {PURPOSE_LABELS[property.purpose]}
              </Badge>
              {property.featured && (
                <Badge className="bg-gold text-white text-xs">Destaque</Badge>
              )}
              {soldOrRented && (
                <Badge className="bg-foreground text-background text-xs">
                  {property.status === 'SOLD' ? 'Vendido' : 'Arrendado'}
                </Badge>
              )}
            </div>
            <h1 className="font-serif text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight sm:text-3xl lg:text-4xl">
              {property.title}
            </h1>
            <div className="mt-2 flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
              <MapPin className="h-4 w-4" />
              <span>
                {property.neighborhood ? `${property.neighborhood}, ` : ''}
                {property.municipality ? `${property.municipality}, ` : ''}
                {property.province || 'Angola'}
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Button
              variant="outline"
              size="icon"
              onClick={handleFavorite}
              className="h-10 w-10"
            >
              <Heart className={cn('h-4 w-4', isFavorite(property.id) && 'fill-gold text-gold')} />
            </Button>
            <Button
              variant="outline"
              size="icon"
              onClick={handleShare}
              className="h-10 w-10"
            >
              <Share2 className="h-4 w-4" />
            </Button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Gallery */}
        <div className="mb-8 grid grid-cols-1 gap-2 lg:grid-cols-3 lg:gap-3">
          {/* Main image */}
          <div
            className="relative aspect-[16/10] cursor-pointer overflow-hidden rounded-sm bg-muted lg:col-span-2 lg:row-span-2"
            onClick={() => setShowGallery(true)}
          >
            <Image
              src={allImages[activeImage] || allImages[0] || '/properties/placeholder.jpg'}
              alt={property.title}
              fill
              priority
              sizes="(max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 66vw"
              className="object-cover transition-transform duration-500 hover:scale-105"
            />
            <div className="absolute bottom-3 right-3 rounded-sm bg-foreground/80 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs text-background backdrop-blur-sm">
              <Camera className="mr-1 inline h-3 w-3" />
              {allImages.length} fotos
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Thumbnails */}
          {allImages.slice(1, 5).map((img, i) => (
            <div
              key={i}
              className="relative aspect-[4/3] cursor-pointer overflow-hidden rounded-sm bg-muted"
              onClick={() => setActiveImage(i + 1)}
            >
              <Image
                src={img}
                alt={`${property.title} - foto ${i + 2}`}
                fill
                sizes="(max-width: 1024px) 50vw, 33vw"
                className="object-cover transition-transform duration-500 hover:scale-105"
              />
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>

        {/* Main content grid */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-8 lg:grid-cols-3">
          {/* Left column — details */}
          <div className="space-y-8 lg:col-span-2">
            {/* Key stats */}
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 rounded-sm border border-border bg-card p-6 sm:grid-cols-4">
              <Stat icon={Bed} label="Quartos" value={property.bedrooms?.toString() ?? '—'} />
              <Stat icon={Bath} label="WCs" value={property.bathrooms?.toString() ?? '—'} />
              <Stat icon={Maximize} label="Área" value={property.area ? formatArea(property.area) : '—'} />
              <Stat icon={Calendar} label="Ano" value={property.year_built?.toString() ?? '—'} />
            </div>

            {/* Description */}
            <div>
              <h2 className="mb-4 font-serif text-xl font-medium">Descrição</h2>
              <div className="prose prose-sm max-w-none text-muted-foreground">
                <p className="whitespace-pre-wrap leading-relaxed">{property.description}</p>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Amenities */}
            {property.amenities && property.amenities.length > 0 && (
              <div>
                <h2 className="mb-4 font-serif text-xl font-medium">Características</h2>
                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-4">
                  {property.amenities.map((amenity) => {
                    const Icon = AMENITY_ICONS[amenity] || Check;
                    return (
                      <div key={amenity} className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm">
                        <div className="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-muted">
                          <Icon className="h-4 w-4 text-gold" />
                        </div>
                        <span>{AMENITY_LABELS[amenity] || amenity}</span>
                      </div>
                    );
                  })}
                </div>
              </div>
            )}

            {/* Location */}
            <div>
              <h2 className="mb-4 font-serif text-xl font-medium">Localização</h2>
              <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-sm border border-border">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between bg-card p-4">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm">
                    <MapPin className="h-4 w-4 text-gold" />
                    <span>
                      {property.neighborhood ? `${property.neighborhood}, ` : ''}
                      {property.municipality ? `${property.municipality}, ` : ''}
                      {property.province}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  {property.address && (
                    <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{property.address}</span>
                  )}
                </div>
                <div className="flex min-h-[200px] items-center justify-center bg-muted/30">
                  <div className="text-center">
                    <MapPin className="mx-auto h-10 w-10 text-muted-foreground/40" />
                    <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground/60">
                      Coordenadas: {property.latitude?.toFixed(4)}, {property.longitude?.toFixed(4)}
                    </p>
                    <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground/40">Mapa interativo em breve</p>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Similar properties */}
            {similar.length > 0 && (
              <div>
                <h2 className="mb-4 font-serif text-xl font-medium">Imóveis semelhantes</h2>
                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
                  {similar.map((p) => (
                    <PropertyCard key={p.id} property={p} />
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Right column — price & contact */}
          <div className="lg:col-span-1">
            <div className="sticky top-24 space-y-4">
              {/* Price card */}
              <div className="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card p-6">
                <div className="mb-1 text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wider text-muted-foreground">
                  {PURPOSE_LABELS[property.purpose]}
                </div>
                <p className="font-serif text-3xl font-bold text-foreground">
                  {formatPrice(property.price, property.currency)}
                </p>
                {property.purpose === 'RENT' && (
                  <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">por mês</p>
                )}
                {property.area && (
                  <p className="mt-3 border-t border-border pt-3 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                    <span className="font-medium text-foreground">
                      {formatPrice(Math.round(property.price / property.area), property.currency)}
                    </span>
                    {' '}/ m²
                  </p>
                )}

                <div className="mt-4 space-y-2">
                  {!soldOrRented ? (
                    <>
                      <Button
                        className="w-full bg-gold text-white hover:bg-gold/90"
                        onClick={() => setShowContact(true)}
                      >
                        <MessageSquare className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" />
                        Contactar anunciante
                      </Button>
                      <Button
                        variant="outline"
                        className="w-full"
                        onClick={() => setShowContact(true)}
                      >
                        <Calendar className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" />
                        Agendar visita
                      </Button>
                    </>
                  ) : (
                    <div className="rounded-sm bg-muted p-4 text-center">
                      <p className="text-sm font-medium">Imóvel já {property.status === 'SOLD' ? 'vendido' : 'arrendado'}</p>
                      <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">Contacte o anunciante para imóveis semelhantes</p>
                      <Button
                        variant="outline"
                        className="mt-3 w-full"
                        onClick={() => setShowContact(true)}
                      >
                        Contactar
                      </Button>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Advertiser card */}
              {ownerProfile && (
                <div className="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card p-6">
                  <div className="mb-3 text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wider text-muted-foreground">
                    Anunciante
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                    <div className="flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-foreground text-background text-sm font-medium">
                      {ownerProfile.full_name?.charAt(0).toUpperCase() ?? 'U'}
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <p className="text-sm font-medium">{ownerProfile.full_name}</p>
                      <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                        {ownerProfile.role === 'AGENT' ? 'Agente imobiliário' :
                         ownerProfile.role === 'AGENCY' ? 'Imobiliária' :
                         'Proprietário'}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {ownerProfile.phone && (
                    <a href={`tel:${ownerProfile.phone}`} className="mt-4 block">
                      <Button variant="outline" className="w-full">
                        <Phone className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" />
                        {ownerProfile.phone}
                      </Button>
                    </a>
                  )}
                </div>
              )}

              {/* Stats */}
              <div className="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card p-6">
                <div className="space-y-3 text-sm">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                    <span className="flex items-center gap-2 text-muted-foreground">
                      <Eye className="h-4 w-4" /> Visualizações
                    </span>
                    <span className="font-medium">{property.view_count}</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                    <span className="flex items-center gap-2 text-muted-foreground">
                      <Heart className="h-4 w-4" /> Favoritos
                    </span>
                    <span className="font-medium">{property.favorite_count}</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                    <span className="flex items-center gap-2 text-muted-foreground">
                      <MessageSquare className="h-4 w-4" /> Mensagens
                    </span>
                    <span className="font-medium">{property.inquiry_count}</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                    <span className="flex items-center gap-2 text-muted-foreground">
                      <Calendar className="h-4 w-4" /> Publicado
                    </span>
                    <span className="font-medium">
                      {property.published_at
                        ? new Date(property.published_at).toLocaleDateString('pt-AO')
                        : new Date(property.created_at).toLocaleDateString('pt-AO')}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Fullscreen gallery */}
      {showGallery && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[100] bg-ink-900/95 backdrop-blur-md">
          <div className="flex h-full flex-col">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-4">
              <span className="text-sm text-white/60">
                {activeImage + 1} / {allImages.length}
              </span>
              <button
                onClick={() => setShowGallery(false)}
                className="rounded-full p-2 text-white hover:bg-white/10"
              >
                <ArrowLeft className="h-5 w-5" />
              </button>
            </div>
            <div className="relative flex flex-1 items-center justify-center px-4">
              <Image
                src={allImages[activeImage] || allImages[0]}
                alt={property.title}
                fill
                sizes="100vw"
                className="object-contain"
              />
              {activeImage > 0 && (
                <button
                  onClick={() => setActiveImage((i) => Math.max(0, i - 1))}
                  className="absolute left-4 rounded-full bg-white/10 p-3 text-white hover:bg-white/20"
                >
                  <ChevronLeft className="h-6 w-6" />
                </button>
              )}
              {activeImage < allImages.length - 1 && (
                <button
                  onClick={() => setActiveImage((i) => Math.min(allImages.length - 1, i + 1))}
                  className="absolute right-4 rounded-full bg-white/10 p-3 text-white hover:bg-white/20"
                >
                  <ChevronRight className="h-6 w-6" />
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      <ContactSellerDialog
        property={property}
        open={showContact}
        onOpenChange={setShowContact}
      />
    </>
  );
}

function Stat({ icon: Icon, label, value }: { icon: React.ComponentType<{ className?: string }>; label: string; value: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="text-center">
      <Icon className="mx-auto h-5 w-5 text-gold" />
      <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground">{label}</p>
      <p className="text-sm font-medium">{value}</p>
    </div>
  );
}

==== project/app/imoveis/comprar/page.tsx ====
'use client';

import { Suspense, useMemo } from 'react';
import { useSearchParams } from 'next/navigation';
import PropertiesPage from '@/app/imoveis/page';
