  return <PropertiesPage />;
}


==== project/app/imoveis/page.tsx ====
'use client';

import { useState, useEffect, useMemo, Suspense } from 'react';
import { useSearchParams, useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { Map as MapIcon, LayoutGrid, ChevronLeft, ChevronRight } from 'lucide-react';
import { useProperties } from '@/lib/use-properties';
import { PropertyCard, PropertyCardSkeleton } from '@/components/property-card';
import { PropertyFiltersBar } from '@/components/property-filters';
import { SiteHeader } from '@/components/site-header';
import { PropertyFilters, DEFAULT_FILTERS, SearchCriteria, SortOption, PropertyPurpose, PropertyType } from '@/lib/types';
import {
  Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue,
} from '@/components/ui/select';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils';

const PAGE_SIZE = 12;

function PropertiesListContent() {
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const router = useRouter();
  const [page, setPage] = useState(1);
  const [showMap, setShowMap] = useState(false);

  // Read initial filters from URL
  const initialFilters = useMemo<PropertyFilters>(() => {
    const purpose = searchParams.get('purpose') as PropertyPurpose | null;
    const type = searchParams.get('type') as PropertyType | null;
    const province = searchParams.get('province');
    const municipality = searchParams.get('municipality');
    const neighborhood = searchParams.get('neighborhood');
    const q = searchParams.get('q');

    return {
      ...DEFAULT_FILTERS,
      purpose: purpose || 'ALL',
      type: type || 'ALL',
      province: province || 'ALL',
      municipality: municipality || 'ALL',
      ...(neighborhood ? {} : {}),
      ...(q ? {} : {}),
    };
  }, [searchParams]);

  const [filters, setFilters] = useState<PropertyFilters>(initialFilters);
  const [sort, setSort] = useState<SortOption>('newest');

  // Update URL when filters change
  useEffect(() => {
    const params = new URLSearchParams();
    if (filters.purpose !== 'ALL') params.set('purpose', filters.purpose);
    if (filters.type !== 'ALL') params.set('type', filters.type);
    if (filters.province !== 'ALL') params.set('province', filters.province);
    if (filters.municipality !== 'ALL') params.set('municipality', filters.municipality);
    if (sort !== 'newest') params.set('sort', sort);

    const url = `/imoveis${params.toString() ? `?${params.toString()}` : ''}`;
    router.replace(url, { scroll: false });
  }, [filters, sort, router]);

  // Reset page when filters change
  useEffect(() => {
    setPage(1);
  }, [filters, sort]);

  // Build search criteria from filters
  const criteria: Partial<SearchCriteria> = useMemo(() => ({
    purpose: filters.purpose !== 'ALL' ? filters.purpose : undefined,
    type: filters.type !== 'ALL' ? filters.type : undefined,
    province: filters.province !== 'ALL' ? filters.province : undefined,
    municipality: filters.municipality !== 'ALL' ? filters.municipality : undefined,
    minPrice: filters.minPrice ? Number(filters.minPrice) : undefined,
    maxPrice: filters.maxPrice ? Number(filters.maxPrice) : undefined,
    minBedrooms: filters.minBedrooms ? Number(filters.minBedrooms) : undefined,
    minBathrooms: filters.minBathrooms ? Number(filters.minBathrooms) : undefined,
    amenities: filters.amenities.length > 0 ? filters.amenities : undefined,
    sort,
  }), [filters, sort]);

  const { properties, total, loading, error } = useProperties({
    criteria,
    page,
    pageSize: PAGE_SIZE,
  });

  const totalPages = Math.ceil(total / PAGE_SIZE);
  const purposeLabel = filters.purpose === 'SALE' ? 'para comprar' :
    filters.purpose === 'RENT' ? 'para arrendar' : '';

  return (
    <>
      <SiteHeader />

      <div className="container-mw container-px py-8 lg:py-12">
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="mb-8">
          <div className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="h-px w-8 bg-gold" />
            <span className="text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-gold">
              Marketplace
            </span>
          </div>
          <h1 className="font-serif text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight sm:text-4xl lg:text-5xl">
            Imóveis {purposeLabel}
          </h1>
          <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
            {loading ? 'A procurar...' : `${total} imóveis disponíveis`}
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Filters */}
        <PropertyFiltersBar
          filters={filters}
          onChange={setFilters}
          resultCount={total}
        />

        {/* Sort & View Toggle */}
        <div className="mt-4 flex items-center justify-between">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Ordenar por:</span>
            <Select value={sort} onValueChange={(v) => setSort(v as SortOption)}>
              <SelectTrigger className="w-44 h-9 text-xs">
                <SelectValue />
              </SelectTrigger>
              <SelectContent>
                <SelectItem value="newest">Mais recentes</SelectItem>
                <SelectItem value="price_asc">Menor preço</SelectItem>
                <SelectItem value="price_desc">Maior preço</SelectItem>
                <SelectItem value="most_viewed">Mais visualizados</SelectItem>
              </SelectContent>
            </Select>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-1 rounded-sm border border-border p-0.5">
            <button
              onClick={() => setShowMap(false)}
              className={cn(
                'flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-sm px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium transition-colors',
                !showMap ? 'bg-foreground text-background' : 'text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground'
              )}
            >
              <LayoutGrid className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              Grelha
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => setShowMap(true)}
              className={cn(
                'flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-sm px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium transition-colors',
                showMap ? 'bg-foreground text-background' : 'text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground'
              )}
            >
              <MapIcon className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              Mapa
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Results */}
        {error ? (
          <div className="mt-12 rounded-sm border border-destructive/20 bg-destructive/5 p-8 text-center">
            <p className="text-sm text-destructive">{error}</p>
          </div>
        ) : showMap ? (
          <MapPlaceholder properties={properties} loading={loading} />
        ) : loading ? (
          <div className="mt-8 grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 xl:grid-cols-4">
            {Array.from({ length: 8 }).map((_, i) => (
              <PropertyCardSkeleton key={i} />
            ))}
          </div>
        ) : properties.length === 0 ? (
          <EmptyState onReset={() => setFilters(DEFAULT_FILTERS)} />
        ) : (
          <>
            <div className="mt-8 grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 xl:grid-cols-4">
              {properties.map((p, i) => (
                <div key={p.id} className="animate-fade-in-up" style={{ animationDelay: `${i * 30}ms` }}>
                  <PropertyCard property={p} />
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>

            {/* Pagination */}
            {totalPages > 1 && (
              <div className="mt-12 flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
                <Button
                  variant="outline"
                  size="sm"
                  onClick={() => setPage((p) => Math.max(1, p - 1))}
                  disabled={page === 1}
                >
                  <ChevronLeft className="h-4 w-4" />
                  Anterior
                </Button>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
                  {Array.from({ length: Math.min(totalPages, 7) }).map((_, i) => {
                    const pageNum = i + 1;
                    const isActive = page === pageNum;
                    return (
                      <button
                        key={pageNum}
                        onClick={() => setPage(pageNum)}
                        className={cn(
                          'flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-sm text-sm font-medium transition-colors',
                          isActive
                            ? 'bg-foreground text-background'
                            : 'text-muted-foreground hover:bg-muted'
                        )}
                      >
                        {pageNum}
                      </button>
                    );
                  })}
                  {totalPages > 7 && (
                    <>
                      <span className="px-2 text-muted-foreground">...</span>
                      <button
                        onClick={() => setPage(totalPages)}
                        className={cn(
                          'flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-sm text-sm font-medium',
                          page === totalPages ? 'bg-foreground text-background' : 'text-muted-foreground hover:bg-muted'
                        )}
                      >
                        {totalPages}
                      </button>
                    </>
                  )}
                </div>
                <Button
                  variant="outline"
                  size="sm"
                  onClick={() => setPage((p) => Math.min(totalPages, p + 1))}
                  disabled={page === totalPages}
                >
                  Próximo
                  <ChevronRight className="h-4 w-4" />
                </Button>
              </div>
            )}
          </>
        )}
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

function MapPlaceholder({ properties, loading }: { properties: ReturnType<typeof useProperties>['properties']; loading: boolean }) {
  return (
    <div className="mt-8 flex min-h-[500px] items-center justify-center rounded-sm border border-border bg-muted/30">
      <div className="text-center">
        <MapIcon className="mx-auto h-12 w-12 text-muted-foreground/40" />
        <p className="mt-4 font-serif text-xl font-medium text-muted-foreground">
          Vista de mapa em breve
        </p>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground/60 max-w-sm">
          A integração com Mapbox está sendo preparada. Cada imóvel já possui coordenadas GPS prontas para visualização no mapa.
        </p>
        <p className="mt-4 text-xs text-muted-foreground/40">
          {loading ? 'A carregar...' : `${properties.length} imóveis com coordenadas prontas`}
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function EmptyState({ onReset }: { onReset: () => void }) {
  return (
    <div className="mt-12 flex flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-sm border border-border bg-card py-20 text-center">
      <div className="flex h-16 w-16 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-muted">
        <MapIcon className="h-8 w-8 text-muted-foreground" />
      </div>
      <h3 className="mt-6 font-serif text-xl font-medium">Nenhum imóvel encontrado</h3>
      <p className="mt-2 max-w-sm text-sm text-muted-foreground">
        Não encontramos imóveis com os filtros selecionados. Tente ajustar os critérios de pesquisa.
      </p>
      <Button onClick={onReset} variant="outline" className="mt-6">
        Limpar filtros
      </Button>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function PropertiesPage() {
  return (
    <Suspense fallback={
      <div className="container-mw container-px py-20">
        <div className="h-8 w-64 animate-pulse rounded bg-muted" />
        <div className="mt-8 grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 xl:grid-cols-4">
          {Array.from({ length: 8 }).map((_, i) => (
            <PropertyCardSkeleton key={i} />
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    }>
      <PropertiesListContent />
    </Suspense>
  );
}


==== project/app/layout.tsx ====

==== project/app/page.tsx ====
'use client';

import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';

  Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue,

const EXPLORE_AREAS = [

export default function HomePage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [searchPurpose, setSearchPurpose] = useState<PropertyPurpose | 'ALL'>('SALE');
  const [searchType, setSearchType] = useState<PropertyType | 'ALL'>('ALL');
  const [searchProvince, setSearchProvince] = useState<string>('ALL');
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');

  const { properties: featured, loading: loadingFeatured } = useProperties({
    featuredOnly: true,
    limit: 6,
  });

  const { properties: newest, loading: loadingNewest } = useProperties({
    limit: 8,
  });

  const handleSearch = () => {
    const params = new URLSearchParams();
    if (searchPurpose !== 'ALL') params.set('purpose', searchPurpose);
    if (searchType !== 'ALL') params.set('type', searchType);
    if (searchProvince !== 'ALL') params.set('province', searchProvince);
    if (searchQuery) params.set('q', searchQuery);
    router.push(`/imoveis?${params.toString()}`);
  };

  return (
    <>
      <SiteHeader />

      {/* ============================================================
          HERO
      ============================================================ */}
      <section className="relative min-h-[100vh] flex items-center overflow-hidden">
        {/* Background */}
        <div className="absolute inset-0 z-0">
          <Image
            src={HERO_IMAGE}
            alt="Luanda skyline"
            fill
            priority
            sizes="100vw"
            className="object-cover"
          />
          <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-b from-ink-900/70 via-ink-900/50 to-ink-900/80" />
        </div>

        {/* Content */}
        <div className="relative z-10 container-mw container-px w-full pt-20 pb-32 lg:pt-32">
          <div className="max-w-3xl">
            <div className="mb-6 flex items-center gap-3 animate-fade-in-down">
              <div className="h-px w-12 bg-gold" />
              <span className="text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-gold">
                Plataforma PropTech de Angola
              </span>
            </div>

            <h1 className="font-serif text-4xl font-bold leading-[1.1] text-white sm:text-5xl lg:text-6xl xl:text-7xl text-balance animate-fade-in-up">
              Encontre onde a sua{' '}
              <span className="text-gold">próxima história</span>{' '}
              começa.
            </h1>

            <p className="mt-6 max-w-xl text-base leading-relaxed text-white/80 sm:text-lg animate-fade-in-up" style={{ animationDelay: '100ms' }}>

            </p>

            <div className="rounded-sm border border-white/20 bg-white/10 p-2 backdrop-blur-md lg:p-3">
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 lg:items-end">
                <div className="bg-white/95 rounded-sm">
                  <div className="px-3 pt-2 pb-1 text-[10px] font-medium uppercase tracking-wider text-muted-foreground">
                    Finalidade
                  </div>
                  <Select value={searchPurpose} onValueChange={(v) => setSearchPurpose(v as PropertyPurpose | 'ALL')}>
                    <SelectTrigger className="border-0 bg-transparent focus:ring-0 text-sm">
                      <SelectValue />
                    </SelectTrigger>
                    <SelectContent>
                      <SelectItem value="ALL">Todas</SelectItem>
                      <SelectItem value="SALE">Comprar</SelectItem>
                      <SelectItem value="RENT">Arrendar</SelectItem>
                      <SelectItem value="LEASE">Arrendamento longo</SelectItem>
                      <SelectItem value="SHORT_TERM">Curta duração</SelectItem>
                    </SelectContent>
                  </Select>
                </div>

                <div className="bg-white/95 rounded-sm">
                  <div className="px-3 pt-2 pb-1 text-[10px] font-medium uppercase tracking-wider text-muted-foreground">
                    Tipo
                  </div>
                  <Select value={searchType} onValueChange={(v) => setSearchType(v as PropertyType | 'ALL')}>
                    <SelectTrigger className="border-0 bg-transparent focus:ring-0 text-sm">
                      <SelectValue />
                    </SelectTrigger>
                    <SelectContent>
                      <SelectItem value="ALL">Todos os tipos</SelectItem>
                      {Object.entries(PROPERTY_TYPE_LABELS).map(([value, label]) => (
                        <SelectItem key={value} value={value}>{label}</SelectItem>
                      ))}
                    </SelectContent>
                  </Select>
                </div>

                <div className="bg-white/95 rounded-sm">
                  <div className="px-3 pt-2 pb-1 text-[10px] font-medium uppercase tracking-wider text-muted-foreground">
                    Localização
                  </div>
                  <Select value={searchProvince} onValueChange={setSearchProvince}>
                    <SelectTrigger className="border-0 bg-transparent focus:ring-0 text-sm">
                      <SelectValue />
                    </SelectTrigger>
                    <SelectContent>
                      <SelectItem value="ALL">Toda Angola</SelectItem>
                      {getProvinces().map((p) => (
                        <SelectItem key={p} value={p}>{p}</SelectItem>
                      ))}
                    </SelectContent>
                  </Select>
                </div>

                <Button
                  onClick={handleSearch}
                  size="lg"
                  className="bg-gold text-white hover:bg-gold/90 h-12 text-sm font-medium"
                >
                  <Search className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" />
                  Pesquisar imóveis
                </Button>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Quick links */}
            <div className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap gap-3">
              <span className="text-xs text-white/60">Pesquisas populares:</span>

          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* ============================================================
          FEATURED PROPERTIES
      ============================================================ */}
      <section className="py-20 lg:py-28">

          <SectionHeading
            tag="Seleção editorial"

          />

          <div className="mt-12 grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
            {loadingFeatured
              ? Array.from({ length: 6 }).map((_, i) => <PropertyCardSkeleton key={i} />)

      {/* ============================================================

                    <span>Explorar</span>

                </div>

      {/* ============================================================

            tag="A jornada"

            description="Da descoberta ao fecho. Cada passo da jornada imobiliária, apoiado por tecnologia."
          />

            {STEPS.map((step, i) => {
              const Icon = step.icon;
              return (

                  key={step.title}
                  className="relative animate-fade-in-up"
                  style={{ animationDelay: `${i * 100}ms` }}
                >
                  {i < STEPS.length - 1 && (
                    <div className="absolute top-8 left-full hidden w-full h-px bg-border lg:block" style={{ width: 'calc(100% - 2rem)', marginLeft: '1rem' }} />
                  )}
                  <div className="flex h-16 w-16 items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-border bg-card">
                    <Icon className="h-7 w-7 text-gold" />
                  </div>
                  <div className="mt-5">
                    <div className="mb-2 text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground">
                      Passo {String(i + 1).padStart(2, '0')}
                    </div>
                    <h3 className="font-serif text-xl font-medium">{step.title}</h3>
                    <p className="mt-2 text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">
                      {step.desc}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>

      </section>

      {/* ============================================================

                <div className="h-px w-12 bg-gold" />

                <div className="h-px w-12 bg-gold" />

              Tem um imóvel para vender ou arrendar?

              Publique o seu imóvel no Kubata Kié e alcance milhares de potenciais compradores e inquilinos em toda Angola. É simples, rápido e gratuito.
            </p>

            <div className="mt-10 flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-4 sm:flex-row">
              <Link href="/publicar">
                <Button size="lg" className="bg-gold text-white hover:bg-gold/90">
                  <PlusCircle className="mr-2 h-5 w-5" />
                  Publicar imóvel

              <Link href="/imoveis">
                <Button size="lg" variant="outline">
                  <Search className="mr-2 h-5 w-5" />
                  Pesquisar imóveis

            </div>

            <div className="mt-12 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-8 text-xs text-muted-foreground">

              </p>

                <span className="text-xs text-gold">Construído em Angola</span>
              </div>
            </div>

            <FooterColumn title="Imóveis" links={[
              { label: 'Comprar', href: '/imoveis/comprar' },

              { label: 'Publicar', href: '/publicar' },

            <FooterColumn title="Plataforma" links={[
              { label: 'Como funciona', href: '/sobre' },

function SectionHeading({
  tag,
  title,
  description,
  action,
}: {
  tag: string;
  title: string;
  description?: string;
  action?: { label: string; href: string };
}) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-end sm:justify-between">
      <div className="max-w-2xl">
        <div className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="h-px w-12 bg-gold" />
          <span className="text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-gold">
            {tag}
          </span>
        </div>
        <h2 className="font-serif text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight sm:text-4xl lg:text-5xl text-balance">
          {title}
        </h2>
        {description && (
          <p className="mt-4 text-base text-muted-foreground sm:text-lg">
            {description}
          </p>
        )}
      </div>
      {action && (
        <Link
          href={action.href}
          className="group inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-medium text-foreground transition-colors hover:text-gold"
        >
          {action.label}
          <ArrowRight className="h-4 w-4 transition-transform group-hover:translate-x-1" />
        </Link>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function FooterColumn({ title, links }: { title: string; links: { label: string; href: string }[] }) {
  return (
    <div>
      <h4 className="mb-4 text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wider text-white/40">{title}</h4>
      <ul className="space-y-2">
        {links.map((link) => (
          <li key={link.href}>
            <Link
              href={link.href}
              className="text-sm text-white/70 transition-colors hover:text-gold"
            >
              {link.label}
            </Link>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
    </div>
  );
}


==== project/app/publicar/page.tsx ====
'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import {
  Check, ChevronLeft, ChevronRight, Upload, X, MapPin,
  Home, Building, TreePine, Store, Warehouse, DollarSign,
  FileText, Camera, Eye, Send,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { useAuth } from '@/lib/auth-context';
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase/client';
import {
  PropertyPurpose, PropertyType, PROPERTY_TYPE_LABELS, PURPOSE_LABELS,
  AMENITY_LABELS, ALL_AMENITIES,
} from '@/lib/types';
import { getProvinces, getMunicipalitiesForProvince } from '@/lib/angola';
import { SiteHeader } from '@/components/site-header';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';
import { Label } from '@/components/ui/label';
import { Textarea } from '@/components/ui/textarea';
import {
  Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue,
} from '@/components/ui/select';
import { toast } from 'sonner';
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils';

const STEPS = [
  { id: 0, label: 'Finalidade', icon: DollarSign },
  { id: 1, label: 'Tipo', icon: Home },
  { id: 2, label: 'Localização', icon: MapPin },
  { id: 3, label: 'Características', icon: Check },
  { id: 4, label: 'Preço', icon: DollarSign },
  { id: 5, label: 'Descrição', icon: FileText },
  { id: 6, label: 'Fotos', icon: Camera },
  { id: 7, label: 'Revisão', icon: Eye },
  { id: 8, label: 'Enviar', icon: Send },
];

const TYPE_ICONS: Record<PropertyType, React.ComponentType<{ className?: string }>> = {
  HOUSE: Home, APARTMENT: Building, LAND: TreePine, OFFICE: Store,
  SHOP: Store, WAREHOUSE: Warehouse, COMMERCIAL: Store,
  DEVELOPMENT: TreePine, OTHER: Building,
};

interface FormData {
  purpose: PropertyPurpose;
  type: PropertyType;
  province: string;
  municipality: string;
  neighborhood: string;
  address: string;
  latitude: string;
  longitude: string;
  bedrooms: string;
  bathrooms: string;
  area: string;
  plotArea: string;
  yearBuilt: string;
  amenities: string[];
  price: string;
  currency: string;
  title: string;
  description: string;
  images: string[];
}

const INITIAL_DATA: FormData = {
  purpose: 'SALE',
  type: 'HOUSE',
  province: 'Luanda',
  municipality: '',
  neighborhood: '',
  address: '',
  latitude: '',
  longitude: '',
  bedrooms: '',
  bathrooms: '',
  area: '',
  plotArea: '',
  yearBuilt: '',
  amenities: [],
  price: '',
  currency: 'AOA',
  title: '',
  description: '',
  images: [],
};

export default function PublishPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const [step, setStep] = useState(0);
  const [data, setData] = useState<FormData>(INITIAL_DATA);
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);

  if (!user) {
    return (
      <>
        <SiteHeader />
        <div className="container-mw container-px py-20 text-center">
          <h1 className="font-serif text-2xl font-bold">Inicie sessão para publicar</h1>
          <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
            Precisa de uma conta para publicar imóveis no Kubata Kié.
          </p>
          <div className="mt-6 flex justify-center gap-3">
            <Button onClick={() => router.push('/auth/entrar')} variant="outline">Entrar</Button>
            <Button onClick={() => router.push('/auth/registar')} className="bg-gold text-white hover:bg-gold/90">
              Criar conta
            </Button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </>
    );
  }

  function update<K extends keyof FormData>(key: K, value: FormData[K]) {
    setData((prev) => ({ ...prev, [key]: value }));
  }

  function toggleAmenity(amenity: string) {
    update('amenities', data.amenities.includes(amenity)
      ? data.amenities.filter((a) => a !== amenity)
      : [...data.amenities, amenity]);
  }

  function next() {
    if (step < STEPS.length - 1) setStep(step + 1);
  }

  function prev() {
    if (step > 0) setStep(step - 1);
  }

  async function handleSubmit() {
    if (!data.title || !data.price || !data.area) {
      toast.error('Preencha título, preço e área');
      return;
    }

    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      const slug = `${data.title.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-').slice(0, 50)}-${Date.now().toString(36)}`;

      const { data: propData, error } = await supabase.from('properties').insert({
        slug,
        title: data.title,
        description: data.description,
        purpose: data.purpose,
        type: data.type,
        price: Number(data.price),
        currency: data.currency,
        bedrooms: data.bedrooms ? Number(data.bedrooms) : null,
        bathrooms: data.bathrooms ? Number(data.bathrooms) : null,
        area: Number(data.area),
        plot_area: data.plotArea ? Number(data.plotArea) : null,
        year_built: data.yearBuilt ? Number(data.yearBuilt) : null,
        province: data.province,
        municipality: data.municipality,
        neighborhood: data.neighborhood,
        address: data.address,
        latitude: data.latitude ? Number(data.latitude) : null,
        longitude: data.longitude ? Number(data.longitude) : null,
        main_image_url: data.images[0] || null,
        images: data.images,
        amenities: data.amenities,
        status: 'PENDING_REVIEW',
        owner_id: user!.id,
      }).select('id').single();

      if (error) throw error;

      toast.success('Imóvel publicado! Será revisto pela nossa equipa.');
      router.push('/dashboard/imoveis');
    } catch (err) {
      toast.error('Erro ao publicar. Tente novamente.');
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  }

  const municipalities = data.province ? getMunicipalitiesForProvince(data.province) : [];

  return (
    <>
      <SiteHeader />
      <div className="container-mw container-px py-8 lg:py-12">
        <div className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="h-px w-8 bg-gold" />
          <span className="text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-gold">
            Publicar imóvel
          </span>
        </div>
        <h1 className="font-serif text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight sm:text-4xl">
          Novo anúncio
        </h1>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
          Passo {step + 1} de {STEPS.length} — {STEPS[step].label}
        </p>

        {/* Step progress */}
        <div className="mt-8 mb-12">
          <div className="hidden lg:flex items-center justify-between">
            {STEPS.map((s, i) => {
              const Icon = s.icon;
              const isComplete = i < step;
              const isActive = i === step;
              return (
                <div key={s.id} className="flex items-center">
                  <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-2">
                    <div
                      className={cn(
                        'flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-full border-2 transition-all',
                        isComplete && 'border-gold bg-gold text-white',
                        isActive && 'border-gold text-gold',
                        !isComplete && !isActive && 'border-border text-muted-foreground'
                      )}
                    >
                      {isComplete ? <Check className="h-5 w-5" /> : <Icon className="h-5 w-5" />}
                    </div>
                    <span className={cn(
                      'text-xs',
                      isActive ? 'font-medium text-foreground' : 'text-muted-foreground'
                    )}>
                      {s.label}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  {i < STEPS.length - 1 && (
                    <div className={cn(
                      'mx-2 h-0.5 w-12 lg:w-16',
                      i < step ? 'bg-gold' : 'bg-border'
                    )} />
                  )}
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
          {/* Mobile progress bar */}
          <div className="lg:hidden">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              {STEPS.map((s, i) => (
                <div
                  key={s.id}
                  className={cn(
                    'h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full transition-all',
                    i <= step ? 'bg-gold' : 'bg-border'
                  )}
                />
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Step content */}
        <div className="mx-auto max-w-2xl">
          {/* Step 0: Purpose */}
          {step === 0 && (
            <StepCard title="Qual é a finalidade do imóvel?">
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                {Object.entries(PURPOSE_LABELS).map(([value, label]) => (
                  <button
                    key={value}
                    onClick={() => update('purpose', value as PropertyPurpose)}
                    className={cn(
                      'rounded-sm border p-4 text-left transition-all',
                      data.purpose === value
                        ? 'border-gold bg-gold/5'
                        : 'border-border hover:border-foreground/20'
                    )}
                  >
                    <p className="font-medium">{label}</p>
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            </StepCard>
          )}

          {/* Step 1: Type */}
          {step === 1 && (
            <StepCard title="Que tipo de imóvel é?">
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-3">
                {Object.entries(PROPERTY_TYPE_LABELS).map(([value, label]) => {
                  const Icon = TYPE_ICONS[value as PropertyType];
                  return (
                    <button
                      key={value}
                      onClick={() => update('type', value as PropertyType)}
                      className={cn(
                        'flex flex-col items-center gap-2 rounded-sm border p-4 transition-all',
                        data.type === value
                          ? 'border-gold bg-gold/5'
                          : 'border-border hover:border-foreground/20'
                      )}
                    >
                      <Icon className="h-6 w-6 text-gold" />
                      <span className="text-sm font-medium">{label}</span>
                    </button>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            </StepCard>
          )}

          {/* Step 2: Location */}
          {step === 2 && (
            <StepCard title="Onde se localiza o imóvel?">
              <div className="space-y-4">
                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                  <div>
                    <Label className="text-xs">Província</Label>
                    <Select value={data.province} onValueChange={(v) => { update('province', v); update('municipality', ''); }}>
                      <SelectTrigger className="mt-1.5"><SelectValue /></SelectTrigger>
                      <SelectContent>
                        {getProvinces().map((p) => (
                          <SelectItem key={p} value={p}>{p}</SelectItem>
                        ))}
                      </SelectContent>
                    </Select>
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <Label className="text-xs">Município</Label>
                    <Select value={data.municipality} onValueChange={(v) => update('municipality', v)}>
                      <SelectTrigger className="mt-1.5"><SelectValue placeholder="Selecione..." /></SelectTrigger>
                      <SelectContent>
                        {municipalities.map((m) => (
                          <SelectItem key={m} value={m}>{m}</SelectItem>
                        ))}
                      </SelectContent>
                    </Select>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <Label htmlFor="neighborhood" className="text-xs">Bairro</Label>
                  <Input id="neighborhood" value={data.neighborhood} onChange={(e) => update('neighborhood', e.target.value)} placeholder="Ex: Talatona" className="mt-1.5" />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <Label htmlFor="address" className="text-xs">Endereço (opcional)</Label>
                  <Input id="address" value={data.address} onChange={(e) => update('address', e.target.value)} placeholder="Rua, número..." className="mt-1.5" />
                </div>
                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                  <div>
                    <Label htmlFor="lat" className="text-xs">Latitude (opcional)</Label>
                    <Input id="lat" value={data.latitude} onChange={(e) => update('latitude', e.target.value)} placeholder="-8.8399" className="mt-1.5" />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <Label htmlFor="lng" className="text-xs">Longitude (opcional)</Label>
                    <Input id="lng" value={data.longitude} onChange={(e) => update('longitude', e.target.value)} placeholder="13.2894" className="mt-1.5" />
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </StepCard>
          )}

          {/* Step 3: Characteristics */}
          {step === 3 && (
            <StepCard title="Características do imóvel">
              <div className="space-y-4">
                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-3">
                  <div>
                    <Label className="text-xs">Quartos</Label>
                    <Input type="number" value={data.bedrooms} onChange={(e) => update('bedrooms', e.target.value)} placeholder="0" className="mt-1.5" />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <Label className="text-xs">WCs</Label>
                    <Input type="number" value={data.bathrooms} onChange={(e) => update('bathrooms', e.target.value)} placeholder="0" className="mt-1.5" />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <Label className="text-xs">Área (m²)</Label>
                    <Input type="number" value={data.area} onChange={(e) => update('area', e.target.value)} placeholder="120" className="mt-1.5" />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <Label className="text-xs">Terreno (m²)</Label>
                    <Input type="number" value={data.plotArea} onChange={(e) => update('plotArea', e.target.value)} placeholder="300" className="mt-1.5" />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <Label className="text-xs">Ano de construção</Label>
                    <Input type="number" value={data.yearBuilt} onChange={(e) => update('yearBuilt', e.target.value)} placeholder="2020" className="mt-1.5" />
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <p className="mb-2 text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground">Características adicionais</p>
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                    {ALL_AMENITIES.map((amenity) => (
                      <button
                        key={amenity}
                        onClick={() => toggleAmenity(amenity)}
                        className={cn(
                          'rounded-full border px-3 py-1.5 text-xs transition-all',
                          data.amenities.includes(amenity)
                            ? 'border-gold bg-gold text-white'
                            : 'border-border text-muted-foreground hover:border-foreground/30'
                        )}
                      >
                        {AMENITY_LABELS[amenity]}
                      </button>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </StepCard>
          )}

          {/* Step 4: Price */}
          {step === 4 && (
            <StepCard title="Preço do imóvel">
              <div className="space-y-4">
                <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-3">
                  <div className="col-span-2">
                    <Label htmlFor="price" className="text-xs">Preço</Label>
                    <Input id="price" type="number" value={data.price} onChange={(e) => update('price', e.target.value)} placeholder="45000000" className="mt-1.5" />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <Label className="text-xs">Moeda</Label>
                    <Select value={data.currency} onValueChange={(v) => update('currency', v)}>
                      <SelectTrigger className="mt-1.5"><SelectValue /></SelectTrigger>
                      <SelectContent>
                        <SelectItem value="AOA">Kz</SelectItem>
                        <SelectItem value="USD">USD</SelectItem>
                        <SelectItem value="EUR">EUR</SelectItem>
                      </SelectContent>
                    </Select>
                  </div>
                </div>
                {data.price && data.area && (
                  <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                    Preço por m²: <span className="font-medium text-foreground">
                      {new Intl.NumberFormat('pt-AO').format(Math.round(Number(data.price) / Number(data.area)))} {data.currency}
                    </span>
                  </p>
                )}
              </div>
            </StepCard>
          )}

          {/* Step 5: Description */}
          {step === 5 && (
            <StepCard title="Descreva o imóvel">
              <div className="space-y-4">
                <div>
                  <Label htmlFor="title" className="text-xs">Título do anúncio</Label>
                  <Input id="title" value={data.title} onChange={(e) => update('title', e.target.value)} placeholder="Ex: Vivenda moderna em Talatona com piscina" className="mt-1.5" />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <Label htmlFor="desc" className="text-xs">Descrição</Label>
                  <Textarea
                    id="desc"
                    value={data.description}
                    onChange={(e) => update('description', e.target.value)}
                    rows={6}
                    placeholder="Descreva o imóvel em detalhe: localização, características, acabamentos, vista..."
                    className="mt-1.5"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>
            </StepCard>
          )}

          {/* Step 6: Photos */}
          {step === 6 && (
            <StepCard title="Fotografias do imóvel">
              <div className="space-y-4">
                <div className="rounded-sm border-2 border-dashed border-border p-8 text-center">
                  <Upload className="mx-auto h-10 w-10 text-muted-foreground/40" />
                  <p className="mt-3 text-sm font-medium">Arraste fotos ou clique para carregar</p>
                  <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">JPG, PNG até 10MB cada</p>
                  <input
                    type="file"
                    multiple
                    accept="image/*"
                    className="hidden"
                    id="photo-upload"
                    onChange={(e) => {
                      const files = e.target.files;
                      if (!files) return;
                      const urls: string[] = [];
                      for (let i = 0; i < files.length; i++) {
                        urls.push(URL.createObjectURL(files[i]));
                      }
                      update('images', [...data.images, ...urls]);
                    }}
                  />
                  <Button
                    type="button"
                    variant="outline"
                    className="mt-4"
                    onClick={() => document.getElementById('photo-upload')?.click()}
                  >
                    <Upload className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" />
                    Selecionar fotos
                  </Button>
                </div>

                {data.images.length > 0 && (
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-3">
                    {data.images.map((img, i) => (
                      <div key={i} className="relative aspect-square overflow-hidden rounded-sm">
                        <img src={img} alt={`Foto ${i + 1}`} className="h-full w-full object-cover" />
                        {i === 0 && (
                          <div className="absolute left-2 top-2 rounded-sm bg-gold px-2 py-0.5 text-xs text-white">
                            Principal
                          </div>
                        )}
                        <button
                          onClick={() => update('images', data.images.filter((_, idx) => idx !== i))}
                          className="absolute right-2 top-2 flex h-6 w-6 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-foreground/80 text-background"
                        >
                          <X className="h-3 w-3" />
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            </StepCard>
          )}

          {/* Step 7: Review */}
          {step === 7 && (
            <StepCard title="Revisão do anúncio">
              <div className="space-y-4 text-sm">
                <ReviewRow label="Finalidade" value={PURPOSE_LABELS[data.purpose]} />
                <ReviewRow label="Tipo" value={PROPERTY_TYPE_LABELS[data.type]} />
                <ReviewRow label="Localização" value={`${data.neighborhood}, ${data.municipality}, ${data.province}`} />
                <ReviewRow label="Área" value={`${data.area} m²`} />
                <ReviewRow label="Quartos" value={data.bedrooms || '—'} />
                <ReviewRow label="WCs" value={data.bathrooms || '—'} />
                <ReviewRow label="Preço" value={`${new Intl.NumberFormat('pt-AO').format(Number(data.price))} ${data.currency}`} />
                <ReviewRow label="Título" value={data.title} />
                <ReviewRow label="Características" value={data.amenities.map(a => AMENITY_LABELS[a]).join(', ') || '—'} />
                <ReviewRow label="Fotos" value={`${data.images.length} foto(s)`} />
              </div>
            </StepCard>
          )}

          {/* Step 8: Submit */}
          {step === 8 && (
            <StepCard title="Enviar para revisão">
              <div className="text-center">
                <div className="mx-auto flex h-16 w-16 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-gold/10">
                  <Send className="h-8 w-8 text-gold" />
                </div>
                <h3 className="mt-4 font-serif text-xl font-medium">Pronto para publicar</h3>
                <p className="mt-2 max-w-md mx-auto text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                  O seu anúncio será enviado para revisão pela nossa equipa. Receberá uma notificação assim que for aprovado e publicado no marketplace.
                </p>
              </div>
            </StepCard>
          )}

          {/* Navigation */}
          <div className="mt-8 flex items-center justify-between">
            <Button variant="outline" onClick={prev} disabled={step === 0}>
              <ChevronLeft className="mr-1 h-4 w-4" />
              Voltar
            </Button>
            {step < STEPS.length - 1 ? (
              <Button onClick={next} className="bg-foreground text-background hover:bg-foreground/90">
                Próximo
                <ChevronRight className="ml-1 h-4 w-4" />
              </Button>
            ) : (
              <Button
                onClick={handleSubmit}
                disabled={submitting}
                className="bg-gold text-white hover:bg-gold/90"
              >
                {submitting ? 'A enviar...' : 'Publicar imóvel'}
                {!submitting && <Send className="ml-2 h-4 w-4" />}
              </Button>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

function StepCard({ title, children }: { title: string; children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-sm border border-border bg-card p-6 lg:p-8 animate-fade-in">
      <h2 className="mb-6 font-serif text-xl font-medium">{title}</h2>
      {children}
    </div>
  );
}

function ReviewRow({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex items-start justify-between border-b border-border/50 pb-3">
      <span className="text-muted-foreground">{label}</span>
      <span className="text-right font-medium">{value}</span>
    </div>
  );
}


==== project/app/sobre/page.tsx ====

            />
          </div>

          <div className="mt-16 rounded-sm border border-border bg-card p-8 text-center">
            <Sparkles className="mx-auto h-8 w-8 text-gold" />
            <h2 className="mt-4 font-serif text-2xl font-medium">Junte-se à plataforma</h2>
            <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
              Crie a sua conta e comece a explorar ou publicar imóveis hoje mesmo.
            </p>
            <div className="mt-6 flex justify-center gap-3">
              <Link href="/auth/registar">
                <Button className="bg-gold text-white hover:bg-gold/90">Criar conta</Button>
              </Link>
              <Link href="/imoveis">
                <Button variant="outline">Explorar imóveis</Button>
              </Link>
            </div>

        </div>
